Documentation:
/**
   * Given a {@link Node}, walk the parent field as far as possible, until
   * reaching the root, which is the {@link Node} for the current
   * representative of this equivalence class. To achieve low runtime
   * complexity, also compress the path, by making each node a direct child of
   * the root.
   */
Method code:
157 |   private Node<E> findRoot(Node<E> node) {
158 |     if (node.parent != node) {
159 |       node.parent = findRoot(node.parent);
160 |     }
161 |     return node.parent;
162 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 122-172
Documentation:

Method code:
122 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
123 |       // null is the smallest value
124 |       if (p2 == null) {
125 |         if (p1 == null) {
126 |           return 0;
127 |         } else {
128 |           return P1_GT_P2;
129 |         }
130 |       }
131 | 
132 |       // check level
133 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
134 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
135 |       }
136 | 
137 |       // sourceName comparison
138 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
139 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
140 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
141 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
142 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
143 |           return sourceCompare;
144 |         }
145 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
146 |         return P1_LT_P2;
147 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
148 |         return P1_GT_P2;
149 |       }
150 |       // lineno comparison
151 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
152 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
153 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
154 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
155 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
156 |         return P1_LT_P2;
157 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
158 |         return P1_GT_P2;
159 |       }
160 |       // charno comparison
161 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
162 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
163 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
164 |         return charno1 - charno2;
165 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
166 |         return P1_LT_P2;
167 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
168 |         return P1_GT_P2;
169 |       }
170 |       // description
171 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
172 |     }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 338-392
Documentation:

Method code:
338 |   protected int getChar() {
339 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
340 |       cursor++;
341 |       --ungetCursor;
342 |       if (charno == -1) {
343 |         charno = getOffset();
344 |       }
345 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     for(;;) {
349 |       int c;
350 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
351 |         hitEOF = true;
352 |         if (charno == -1) {
353 |           charno = getOffset();
354 |         }
355 |         return EOF_CHAR;
356 |       }
357 |       cursor++;
358 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
359 | 
360 | 
361 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
362 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
363 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
364 |           continue;
365 |         }
366 |         lineEndChar = -1;
367 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
368 |         lineno++;
369 |       }
370 | 
371 |       if (c <= 127) {
372 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
373 |           lineEndChar = c;
374 |           c = '\n';
375 |         }
376 |       } else {
377 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
378 |           continue;
379 |         }
380 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
381 |           lineEndChar = c;
382 |           c = '\n';
383 |         }
384 |       }
385 | 
386 |       if (charno == -1) {
387 |         charno = getOffset();
388 |       }
389 | 
390 |       return c;
391 |     }
392 |   }

155 |   @Override
156 |   public List<GraphEdge<N, E>> getEdges(N n1, N n2) {
157 |     // Since this is a method from a generic graph, edges from both
158 |     // directions must be added to the returning list.
159 |     List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> forwardEdges = getDirectedGraphEdges(n1, n2);
160 |     List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> backwardEdges = getDirectedGraphEdges(n2, n1);
161 |     int totalSize = forwardEdges.size() + backwardEdges.size();
162 |     List<GraphEdge<N, E>> edges = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(totalSize);
163 |     edges.addAll(forwardEdges);
164 |     edges.addAll(backwardEdges);
165 |     return edges;
166 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 457-484
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
457 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
458 |     {
459 |         final String defaultResource
460 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
461 | 
462 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
463 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
464 | 
465 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
466 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
467 | 
468 |         String formatString;
469 |         try {
470 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
471 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
472 |             throw new RuntimeException
473 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
474 |         }
475 | 
476 |         /*
477 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
478 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
479 |          * single 's.
480 |          */
481 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
482 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1109-1117
Documentation:
/**
     * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
     * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
     * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
     * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
     */
Method code:
1109 |     protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1110 |       if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1111 |         return -1;
1112 |       } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1113 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1114 |       } else {
1115 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1116 |       }
1117 |     }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isControlStructureCodeBlock(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 955-979
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is code node for FOR, DO,
   * WHILE, WITH, or IF node.
   */
Method code:
955 |   static boolean isControlStructureCodeBlock(Node parent, Node n) {
956 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
957 |       case Token.FOR:
958 |       case Token.WHILE:
959 |       case Token.LABEL:
960 |       case Token.WITH:
961 |         return parent.getLastChild() == n;
962 |       case Token.DO:
963 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n;
964 |       case Token.IF:
965 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != n;
966 |       case Token.TRY:
967 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n || parent.getLastChild() == n;
968 |       case Token.CATCH:
969 |         return parent.getLastChild() == n;
970 |       case Token.SWITCH:
971 |       case Token.CASE:
972 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != n;
973 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
974 |         return true;
975 |       default:
976 |         Preconditions.checkState(isControlStructure(parent));
977 |         return false;
978 |     }
979 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.connect(N,E,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 52-71
Documentation:

Method code:
52 |   @Override
53 |   public GraphEdge<N, E> connect(N srcValue, E edgeValue, N destValue) {
54 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> node = getDirectedGraphNode(srcValue);
55 |     if (node == null) {
56 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
57 |           srcValue + " does not exist in graph");
58 |     }
59 |     LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E> src = (LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E>) node;
60 |     node = getDirectedGraphNode(destValue);
61 |     if (node == null) {
62 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
63 |           destValue + " does not exist in graph");
64 |     }
65 |     LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E> dest = (LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E>) node;
66 |     LinkedDirectedGraphEdge<N, E> edge =
67 |         new LinkedDirectedGraphEdge<N, E>(src, edgeValue, dest);
68 |     src.getOutEdges().add(edge);
69 |     dest.getInEdges().add(edge);
70 |     return edge;
71 |   }

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 337-355
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
337 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
338 |     if (prototype == null) {
339 |       return false;
340 |     }
341 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
342 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
343 |       return false;
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     this.prototype = prototype;
347 | 
348 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
349 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
350 |       if (superClass != null) {
351 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 |     return true;
355 |   }

===== M0067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 627-702
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
627 |   static int precedence(int type) {
628 |     switch (type) {
629 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
630 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
631 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
632 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
633 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
634 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
635 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
636 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
637 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
638 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
639 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
640 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
641 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
642 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
643 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
644 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
645 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
646 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
647 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
648 |       case Token.EQ:
649 |       case Token.NE:
650 |       case Token.SHEQ:
651 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
652 |       case Token.LT:
653 |       case Token.GT:
654 |       case Token.LE:
655 |       case Token.GE:
656 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
657 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
658 |       case Token.LSH:
659 |       case Token.RSH:
660 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
661 |       case Token.SUB:
662 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
663 |       case Token.MUL:
664 |       case Token.MOD:
665 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
666 |       case Token.INC:
667 |       case Token.DEC:
668 |       case Token.NEW:
669 |       case Token.DELPROP:
670 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
671 |       case Token.VOID:
672 |       case Token.NOT:
673 |       case Token.BITNOT:
674 |       case Token.POS:
675 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
676 | 
677 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
678 |       case Token.CALL:
679 |       case Token.EMPTY:
680 |       case Token.FALSE:
681 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
682 |       case Token.GETELEM:
683 |       case Token.GETPROP:
684 |       case Token.GET_REF:
685 |       case Token.IF:
686 |       case Token.LP:
687 |       case Token.NAME:
688 |       case Token.NULL:
689 |       case Token.NUMBER:
690 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
691 |       case Token.REGEXP:
692 |       case Token.RETURN:
693 |       case Token.STRING:
694 |       case Token.THIS:
695 |       case Token.TRUE:
696 |         return 15;
697 | 
698 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
699 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
700 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
701 |     }
702 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java

Method code:
452 |     @Override
453 |     protected void initialize() {
454 |       orderedWorkSet.clear();
455 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node : getCfg().getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
456 |         List<DiGraphEdge<N, Branch>> edgeList =
457 |           getCfg().getOutEdges(node.getValue());
458 |         int outEdgeCount = edgeList.size();

466 |           orderedWorkSet.add(node);
467 |         }
468 |       }

===== M0072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1104-1133
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1104 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1105 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1106 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1107 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1108 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1109 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1110 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1111 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1112 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1113 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1114 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1115 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1116 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1117 |       return false;
1118 |     }
1119 | 
1120 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1121 |     if (type == null) {
1122 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1123 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1124 |       if (var != null) {
1125 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1126 |         if (varType != null) {
1127 |           type = varType;
1128 |         }
1129 |       }
1130 |     }
1131 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1132 |     return true;
1133 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1143-1169
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1143 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1144 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1145 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1146 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1147 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1148 |       return;
1149 |     }
1150 | 
1151 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1152 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1153 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1154 |     // and what isn't.
1155 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1156 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1157 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1158 | 
1159 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1160 |     // non-object.
1161 |     if (!validator.expectNotVoid(t, n, childType,
1162 |             "undefined has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1163 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1164 |       return;
1165 |     }
1166 | 
1167 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1168 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1169 |   }

   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.

190 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
191 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
192 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
193 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
194 |         objType.isInterface()) {
195 |       // Named types.

199 | 
200 |         if (objType.isConstructor() || objType.isInterface()) {
201 |           ((FunctionType) objType).getInstanceType().setJSDocInfo(
202 |               docInfo);
203 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
204 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
205 |         }
206 |       }
207 |     } else if (!objType.isNativeObjectType() &&
208 |         objType.isFunctionType()) {
209 |       // Structural functions.
210 |       objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
211 |     }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 275-308
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
275 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
276 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
277 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
278 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
279 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(info.getThisType().evaluate(scope));
280 |     }
281 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
282 |       // TODO(user): Doing an instanceof check here is too
283 |       // restrictive as (Date,Error) is, for instance, an object type
284 |       // even though its implementation is a UnionType. Would need to
285 |       // create interfaces JSType, ObjectType, FunctionType etc and have
286 |       // separate implementation instead of the class hierarchy, so that
287 |       // union types can also be object types, etc.
288 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
289 |     } else if (owner != null &&
290 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
291 |       // If the function is of the form:
292 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
293 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
294 |       // if it's of the form:
295 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
296 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
297 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
298 |       ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(
299 |           typeRegistry.getType(
300 |               scope, ownerTypeName, sourceName,
301 |               owner.getLineno(), owner.getCharno()));
302 |       if (ownerType != null) {
303 |         thisType = ownerType;
304 |       }
305 |     }
306 | 
307 |     return this;
308 |   }

===== M0115 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.getDirectedGraphEdges(N,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 173-190
Documentation:

Method code:
173 |   @Override
174 |   public List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> getDirectedGraphEdges(N n1, N n2) {
175 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = nodes.get(n1);
176 |     if (dNode1 == null) {
177 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(n1 + " does not exist in graph");
178 |     }
179 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = nodes.get(n2);
180 |     if (dNode2 == null) {
181 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(n1 + " does not exist in graph");
182 |     }
183 |     List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> edges = Lists.newArrayList();
184 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
185 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2) {
186 |         edges.add(outEdge);
187 |       }
188 |     }
189 |     return edges;
190 |   }

===== M0146 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 129-175
Documentation:

Method code:
129 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
130 |                                    String sourceString,
131 |                                    Config config,
132 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
133 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
134 |         config, errorReporter);
135 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
136 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
137 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
138 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
139 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
140 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
141 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
142 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
143 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
144 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
145 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
146 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
147 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
148 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
149 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
150 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
151 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
152 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
153 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
154 |           } else {
155 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
156 |             if (info != null) {
157 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
158 |             }
159 |           }
160 |         }
161 |       }
162 | 
163 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
164 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
165 |       // to that node.
166 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
167 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
168 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
169 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
170 |         }
171 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |     return irNode;
175 |   }

===== M0153 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.Graph.connectIfNotFound(N,E,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/Graph.java
Original range: 121-138
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects two nodes in the graph with an edge if such edge does not already
   * exists between the nodes.
   *
   * @param n1 First node.
   * @param edge The edge.
   * @param n2 Second node.
   */
Method code:
121 |   public final void connectIfNotFound(N n1, E edge, N n2) {
122 |     GraphNode<N, E> node1 = getNode(n1);
123 |     GraphNode<N, E> node2 = getNode(n2);
124 |     if (node1 == null) {
125 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(n1 + " does not exist in graph");
126 |     }
127 |     if (node2 == null) {
128 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(n2 + " does not exist in graph");
129 |     }
130 |     List<GraphEdge<N, E>> edgeList = getEdges(n1, n2);
131 |     for (GraphEdge<N, E> e : edgeList) {
132 |       // Check == first to short circuit nulls.
133 |       if (e.getValue() == edge || e.getValue().equals(edge)) {
134 |         return;
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 |     connect(n1, edge, n2);
138 |   }

582 |         child.next = null;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1396-1412
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1396 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1397 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1398 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1399 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1400 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1401 |         next();
1402 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1403 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1404 |           next();
1405 |         }
1406 |         skipEOLs();
1407 |         token = next();
1408 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1409 |       }
1410 |     }
1411 |     return typeExpr;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DisambiguateProperties.renameProperties()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DisambiguateProperties.java
Original range: 471-507
Documentation:
/** Renames all properties with references on more than one type. */
Method code:
471 |   void renameProperties() {
472 |     int propsRenamed = 0, propsSkipped = 0, instancesRenamed = 0,
473 |         instancesSkipped = 0, singleTypeProps = 0;
474 | 
475 |     for (Property prop : properties.values()) {
476 |       if (prop.shouldRename()) {
477 |         Map<T, String> propNames = buildPropNames(prop.getTypes(), prop.name);
478 | 
479 |         ++propsRenamed;
480 |         prop.expandTypesToSkip();
481 |         UnionFind<T> types = prop.getTypes();
482 |         for (Node node : prop.renameNodes) {
483 |           T rootType = prop.rootTypes.get(node);
484 |           if (prop.shouldRename(rootType)) {
485 |             String newName = propNames.get(rootType);
486 |             node.setString(newName);
487 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
488 |             ++instancesRenamed;
489 |           } else {
490 |             ++instancesSkipped;
491 |           }
492 |         }
493 |       } else {
494 |         if (prop.skipRenaming) {
495 |           ++propsSkipped;
496 |         } else {
497 |           ++singleTypeProps;
498 |         }
499 |       }
500 |     }
501 |     logger.info("Renamed " + instancesRenamed + " instances of "
502 |                 + propsRenamed + " properties.");
503 |     logger.info("Skipped renaming " + instancesSkipped + " invalidated "
504 |                 + "properties, " + propsSkipped + " instances of properties "
505 |                 + "that were skipped for specific types and " + singleTypeProps
506 |                 + " properties that were referenced from only one type.");
507 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1543-1577
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1543 |     public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1544 |         if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1545 |             try {
1546 |                 int[] indices1 = (int[])getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1547 |                 int[] indices2 = (int[])node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1548 |                 if (indices1 == null) {
1549 |                     if (indices2 != null)
1550 |                         return false;
1551 |                 } else if (indices2 == null) {
1552 |                     return false;
1553 |                 } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1554 |                     return false;
1555 |                 } else {
1556 |                     for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1557 |                         if (indices1[i] != indices2[i])
1558 |                             return false;
1559 |                     }
1560 |                 }
1561 |             } catch (Exception e) {
1562 |                 return false;
1563 |             }
1564 |         } else if (type == Token.INC ||
1565 |                    type == Token.DEC) {
1566 |             int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1567 |             int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1568 |             if (post1 != post2)
1569 |                 return false;
1570 |         } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1571 |             int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1572 |             int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1573 |             if (quoted1 != quoted2)
1574 |                 return false;
1575 |         }
1576 |         return true;
1577 |     }

947 |                       value = "last local block";

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DisambiguateProperties.JSTypeSystem.getTypeWithProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DisambiguateProperties.java
Original range: 736-767
Documentation:

Method code:
736 |     @Override public ObjectType getTypeWithProperty(String field, JSType type) {
737 |       if (!(type instanceof ObjectType)) {
738 |         if (type.autoboxesTo() != null) {
739 |           type = type.autoboxesTo();
740 |         } else {
741 |           return null;
742 |         }
743 |       }
744 | 
745 |       // Ignore the prototype itself at all times.
746 |       if ("prototype".equals(field)) {
747 |         return null;
748 |       }
749 | 
750 |       // We look up the prototype chain to find the highest place (if any) that
751 |       // this appears.  This will make references to overriden properties look
752 |       // like references to the initial property, so they are renamed alike.
753 |       ObjectType foundType = null;
754 |       ObjectType objType = ObjectType.cast(type);
755 |       while (objType != null && objType.getImplicitPrototype() != objType) {
756 |         if (objType.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
757 |           foundType = objType;
758 |         }
759 |         objType = objType.getImplicitPrototype();
760 |       }
761 |       // If the property does not exist on the referenced type but the original
762 |       // type is an object type, see if any subtype has the property.
763 |         // getGreatestSubtypeWithProperty does not guarantee that the property
764 |         // is defined on the returned type, it just indicates that it might be,
765 |         // so we have to double check.
766 |       return foundType;
767 |     }

/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
302 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
303 |       Node ret = transform(n);
304 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
305 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
306 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
307 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
308 |       }
309 |       return ret;
310 |     }

506 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {

/**
     * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair
     * of nodes that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal.
     * Package private for testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
     */
Method code:
1350 |     NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1351 |         boolean eq = false;
1352 | 
1353 |         if (type == node2.getType() &&
1354 |             getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount() &&
1355 |             getClass() == node2.getClass()) {
1356 | 
1357 |             eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1358 |         }

1362 |         }
1363 |

1365 |         Node n, n2;
1366 |         for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1367 |              res == null && n != null;
1368 |              n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1369 |             res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1370 |             if (res != null) {
1371 |               return res;
1372 |             }
1373 |         }
1374 |         return res;

===== M0195 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 145-186
Documentation:

Method code:
145 |   @Override
146 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
147 |     this.root = root;
148 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
149 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
150 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
151 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities);
152 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
153 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
154 | 
155 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
156 |     // search on the CFG.
157 |     priorityCounter = 0;
158 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
159 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
160 | 
161 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
162 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
163 |       // priority of them too.
164 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
165 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
166 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
167 |           Preconditions.checkState(
168 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
169 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
170 |         }
171 |       }
172 |     }
173 | 
174 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
175 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
176 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
177 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
178 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
179 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
180 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
181 |       }
182 |     }
183 | 
184 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
185 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
186 |   }

   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.

692 |     private Set<JSType> getTypesToSkipForTypeNonUnion(JSType type) {

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-81
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     if (!Character.isUpperCase(name.charAt(0))) {
76 |       return false;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // hack way of checking that there aren't any lower-case letters
80 |     return name.toUpperCase().equals(name);
81 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 428-458
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
428 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
429 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
430 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
431 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
432 |     if (isOptional) {
433 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
434 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
435 |       // error can be reported.
436 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
437 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
438 |         emittedWarning = true;
439 |       }
440 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
441 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
442 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
443 |         emittedWarning = true;
444 |       }
445 |     } else {
446 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
447 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
448 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
449 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
450 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
451 |         } else {
452 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
453 |         }
454 |         emittedWarning = true;
455 |       }
456 |     }
457 |     return emittedWarning;
458 |   }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 478-520
Documentation:

Method code:
478 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
479 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
480 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
481 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
482 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
483 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
484 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
485 |       return;
486 |     }
487 | 
488 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
489 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
490 | 
491 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
492 |     // function (unless it is called)
493 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
494 |       child = child.getNext();
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     if (child != null) {
498 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
499 |     } else {
500 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node));
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     // Synthetic blocks
504 |     if (parent != null) {
505 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
506 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
507 |         case Token.CASE:
508 |         case Token.TRY:
509 |           break;
510 |         default:
511 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
512 |             Node next = node.getLastChild();
513 |             if (next != null) {
514 |               createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFallThrough(next));
515 |             }
516 |           }
517 |           break;
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |   }

===== M0222 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processTryStatement(TryStatement)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 743-773
Documentation:

Method code:
743 |     @Override
744 |     Node processTryStatement(TryStatement statementNode) {
745 |       Node node = new Node(Token.TRY, transform(statementNode.getTryBlock()));
746 |       Node block = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
747 |       boolean lineSet = false;
748 | 
749 |       for (CatchClause cc : statementNode.getCatchClauses()) {
750 |         // Mark the enclosing block at the same line as the first catch
751 |         // clause.
752 |         if (lineSet == false) {
753 |             block.setLineno(cc.getLineno());
754 |             lineSet = true;
755 |         }
756 |         block.addChildToBack(transform(cc));
757 |       }
758 |       node.addChildToBack(block);
759 | 
760 |       AstNode finallyBlock = statementNode.getFinallyBlock();
761 |       if (finallyBlock != null) {
762 |         node.addChildToBack(transform(finallyBlock));
763 |       }
764 | 
765 |       // If we didn't set the line on the catch clause, then
766 |       // we've got an empty catch clause.  Set its line to be the same
767 |       // as the finally block (to match Old Rhino's behavior.)
768 |       if ((lineSet == false) && (finallyBlock != null)) {
769 |         block.setLineno(finallyBlock.getLineno());
770 |       }
771 | 
772 |       return node;
773 |     }

===== M0230 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 261-289
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
261 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
262 |     initialize();
263 |     int step = 0;
264 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
265 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
266 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
267 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
268 |       }
269 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
270 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
271 |       joinInputs(curNode);
272 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
273 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
274 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
275 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
276 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
277 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
278 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
279 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
280 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
281 |           }
282 |         }
283 |       }
284 |       step++;
285 |     }

===== M0240 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 958-992
Documentation:

Method code:
958 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
959 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
960 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
961 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
962 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
963 |     if (var != null) {
964 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
965 |       if (varType != null) {
966 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
967 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
968 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
969 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
970 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
971 |         } else {
972 |           return varType;
973 |         }
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 | 
977 |     JSType propertyType = null;
978 |     if (objType != null) {
979 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
980 |     }
981 | 
982 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
983 |         qualifiedName != null) {
984 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
985 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
986 |       if (regType != null) {
987 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
988 |       }
989 |     }
990 | 
991 |     return propertyType;
992 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any

===== M0244 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 382-424
Documentation:

Method code:
382 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
383 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
384 |     JSDocInfo info;
385 |     switch (n.getType()) {
386 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
387 |       case Token.VAR:
388 |         // @notypecheck
389 |         info = n.getJSDocInfo();
390 |         if (info != null && info.isNoTypeCheck()) {
391 |           return false;
392 |         }
393 |         break;
394 | 
395 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
396 |         // @notypecheck
397 |         info = n.getJSDocInfo();
398 |         info = (info == null) ? parent.getJSDocInfo() : info;
399 |         if (info != null && info.isNoTypeCheck()) {
400 |           return false;
401 |         }
402 | 
403 |         // normal type checking
404 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
405 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
406 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
407 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
408 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
409 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
410 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
411 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
412 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
413 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
414 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
415 |           t.report(n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
416 |         }
417 | 
418 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
419 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
420 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
421 |         break;
422 |     }
423 |     return true;
424 |   }

278 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
279 |     switch (n.getType()) {

288 |       case Token.GETPROP:

291 |

293 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
294 |             .createChildFlowScope();
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.OR:
298 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
299 |             .createChildFlowScope();
300 |         break;

303 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
304 |         break;
305 | 
306 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
307 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
308 |         break;
309 | 
310 |       case Token.CALL:
311 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
312 |         break;
313 | 
314 |       case Token.NEW:
315 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
316 |         break;
317 | 
318 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
319 |       case Token.ADD:
320 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
321 |         break;
322 |

324 |       case Token.NEG:
325 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
326 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));

331 |         break;

335 |         break;

339 |         break;

342 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
343 |         break;
344 | 
345 |       case Token.REGEXP:
346 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
347 |         break;
348 | 
349 |       case Token.THIS:
350 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
351 |         break;
352 |

354 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
355 |       case Token.LSH:
356 |       case Token.RSH:
357 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
358 |       case Token.URSH:
359 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:

361 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
362 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
363 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
364 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
365 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
366 |       case Token.DIV:
367 |       case Token.MOD:
368 |       case Token.BITAND:
369 |       case Token.BITXOR:
370 |       case Token.BITOR:
371 |       case Token.MUL:
372 |       case Token.SUB:

377 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
378 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
379 |         break;
380 |

382 |       case Token.GET_REF:
383 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
384 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
385 |         break;
386 | 
387 |       case Token.COMMA:
388 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
389 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
390 |         break;
391 | 
392 |       case Token.STRING:
393 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
394 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
395 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
396 |         break;
397 | 
398 |       case Token.LT:
399 |       case Token.LE:
400 |       case Token.GT:

402 |       case Token.NOT:

406 |       case Token.SHNE:
407 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:

412 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
413 |         break;
414 | 
415 |       case Token.GETELEM:
416 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
417 |         break;
418 | 
419 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
420 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
421 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
422 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
423 |         }
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.SWITCH:
427 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
428 |         break;

431 |       case Token.RETURN:
432 |       case Token.THROW:
433 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
434 |         break;
435 | 
436 |       case Token.CATCH:
437 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
438 |         break;
439 |     }
440 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
441 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
442 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
443 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope);
444 | 
445 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
446 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
447 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
448 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&

450 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
451 |         }
452 |

454 |       }
455 |     }
456 | 
457 |     return scope;
458 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 369-384
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
369 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
370 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
371 |       Set<String> directives = null;
372 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
373 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
374 |         if (directives == null) {
375 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
376 |         } else {
377 |           directives.add(directive);
378 |         }
379 |       }
380 | 
381 |       if (directives != null) {
382 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
383 |       }
384 |     }

===== M0248 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DisambiguateProperties.FindRenameableProperties.processProperty(NodeTraversal,Property,T,T)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DisambiguateProperties.java
Original range: 442-467
Documentation:
/**
     * Processes a property, adding it to the list of properties to rename.
     * @return a representative type for the property reference, which will be
     *   the highest type on the prototype chain of the provided type.  In the
     *   case of a union type, it will be the highest type on the prototype
     *   chain of one of the members of the union.
     */
Method code:
442 |     private T processProperty(
443 |         NodeTraversal t, Property prop, T type, T relatedType) {
444 |       type = typeSystem.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined(type);
445 |       if (prop.skipRenaming || typeSystem.isInvalidatingType(type)) {
446 |         return null;
447 |       }
448 | 
449 |       Iterable<T> alternatives = typeSystem.getTypeAlternatives(type);
450 |       if (alternatives != null) {
451 |         T firstType = null;
452 |         for (T subType : alternatives) {
453 |           T lastType = processProperty(t, prop, subType, firstType);
454 |           if (lastType != null) {
455 |             firstType = firstType == null ? lastType : firstType;
456 |           }
457 |         }
458 |         return firstType;
459 |       } else {
460 |         T topType = typeSystem.getTypeWithProperty(prop.name, type);
461 |         if (typeSystem.isInvalidatingType(topType)) {
462 |           return null;
463 |         }
464 |         prop.addType(type, topType, relatedType);
465 |         return topType;
466 |       }
467 |     }

400 |                                  processProperty(t, prop, type, null))) {

Method code:
428 |     @Override
429 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
430 |       if (lineBreak) {
431 |         if (sawFunction) {
432 |           startNewLine();
433 |           sawFunction = false;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 | 
437 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
438 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
439 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
440 |       int len = code.length();
441 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
442 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
443 |         if (ch == ';') {
444 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
445 |         }
446 |       }
447 |       maybeCutLine();
448 |     }

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 177-207
Documentation:

Method code:
177 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
178 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
179 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;
180 |     if (jsDoc != null) {
181 |       nodeWithJsDoc = new NodeWithJsDoc();
182 |       nodesWithJsDoc.put(jsDoc, nodeWithJsDoc);
183 |     }
184 | 
185 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
186 |     if (nodeWithJsDoc != null) {
187 |       nodeWithJsDoc.node = irNode;
188 |     }
189 | 
190 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
191 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
192 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
193 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
194 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
195 |     } else {
196 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
197 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
198 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
199 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
200 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
201 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
202 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
203 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
204 |       }
205 |     }
206 |     return irNode;
207 |   }

/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1501 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1502 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1503 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1504 |     }
1505 | 
1506 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1507 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1508 |       next();
1509 |       skipEOLs();
1510 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1511 |       if (memberType != null) {
1512 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1513 | 
1514 |         skipEOLs();
1515 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1516 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1517 |         }
1518 | 
1519 |         next();
1520 |       }
1521 |     }
1522 |     return typeName;
1523 |   }

===== M0273 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 149-194
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
149 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
150 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
151 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
152 |       case Token.BLOCK:
153 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
154 |       case Token.TRY:
155 |       case Token.FINALLY:
156 |         return true;
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
159 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
160 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
161 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
162 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
163 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
164 |         // child.
165 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
166 |       case Token.WHILE:
167 |       case Token.DO:
168 |       case Token.IF:
169 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
170 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
171 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
172 |         
173 |       case Token.FOR:
174 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
175 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
176 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
177 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
178 |         // That way the following:
179 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
180 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
181 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
182 |           return n == parent.getLastChild(); 
183 |         } else {
184 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
185 |         }
186 |       case Token.SWITCH:
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.WITH:
190 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
191 |       default:
192 |         return false;
193 |     }
194 |   }

269 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
270 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
271 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
272 |       return position;
273 |     } else {
274 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
275 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
276 |     }
277 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 611-637
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
611 |   @Override
612 |   public boolean equals(Object otherType) {
613 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
614 |       return false;
615 |     }
616 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
617 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
618 |       return false;
619 |     }
620 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
621 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
622 |         return this == that;
623 |       }
624 |       return false;
625 |     }
626 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
627 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
628 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
629 |       }
630 |       return false;
631 |     }
632 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
633 |       return false;
634 |     }
635 |     return this.typeOfThis.equals(that.typeOfThis) &&
636 |         this.call.equals(that.call);
637 |   }

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 946-1020
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
946 |   Node parseInputs() {
947 |     boolean devMode = options_.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
948 | 
949 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
950 |     // individual file parse trees.
951 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
952 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
953 |     }
954 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
955 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
956 |     }
957 | 
958 |     // Parse main js sources.
959 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
960 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
961 | 
962 |     if (options_.tracer.isOn()) {
963 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
964 |           options_.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
965 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
966 |     }
967 | 
968 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
969 | 
970 |     try {
971 |       // Parse externs sources.
972 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
973 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
974 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs_) {
975 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
976 |         if (hasErrors()) {
977 |           return null;
978 |         }
979 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
980 |       }
981 | 
982 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs_) {
983 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
984 |         if (hasErrors()) {
985 |           return null;
986 |         }
987 | 
988 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
989 |         if (n == null) {
990 |           continue;
991 |         }
992 | 
993 |         if (devMode) {
994 |           runSanityCheck();
995 |           if (hasErrors()) {
996 |             return null;
997 |           }
998 |         }
999 | 
1000 |         if (options_.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1001 |             options_.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1002 | 
1003 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1004 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1005 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1006 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(input.getName());
1007 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1008 |         }
1009 | 
1010 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1011 |       }
1012 | 
1013 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1014 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1015 | 
1016 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1017 |     } finally {
1018 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1019 |     }
1020 |   }

1197 |             // We have already used the current object as an iterator;
1198 |             // we must create a new SiblingNodeIterable based on this
1199 |             // iterable's start node.
1200 |             //
1201 |             // Since the primary use case for Node.children is in for
1202 |             // loops, this branch is extremely unlikely.
1203 |             return (new SiblingNodeIterable(start)).iterator();
1204 |           }
1205 |         }

===== M0294 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.toString()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 654-696
Documentation:
/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
654 |   @Override
655 |   public String toString() {
656 |     if (this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
657 |       return "Function";
658 |     }
659 | 
660 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
661 |     b.append("function (");
662 |     int paramNum = (call == null || call.parameters == null) ?
663 |         0 : call.parameters.getChildCount();
664 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !typeOfThis.isUnknownType();
665 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
666 |       b.append("this:");
667 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toString());
668 |     }
669 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
670 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
671 |         b.append(", ");
672 |       }
673 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
674 |       if (p.isVarArgs()) {
675 |         appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
676 |       } else {
677 |         b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
678 |       }
679 |       p = p.getNext();
680 |       while (p != null) {
681 |         b.append(", ");
682 |         if (p.isVarArgs()) {
683 |           appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
684 |         } else {
685 |           b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
686 |         }
687 |         p = p.getNext();
688 |       }
689 |     }
690 |     b.append(")");
691 |     if (call != null && call.returnType != null) {
692 |       b.append(": ");
693 |       b.append(call.returnType);
694 |     }
695 |     return b.toString();
696 |   }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 489-535
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
489 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
490 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
491 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
492 |     switch (left.getType()) {
493 |       case Token.NAME:
494 |         String varName = left.getString();
495 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
496 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
497 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
498 |         }
499 | 
500 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
501 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
502 |         // This has two purposes:
503 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
504 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
505 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
506 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
507 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
508 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
509 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
510 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
511 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
512 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
513 |         }
514 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
515 |             resultType : null);
516 | 
517 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
518 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
519 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
520 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
521 |         }
522 |         break;
523 |       case Token.GETPROP:
524 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
525 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
526 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
527 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
528 |               resultType);
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
532 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
533 |         break;
534 |     }
535 |   }
