===== M0302 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   */
Method code:
1444 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1445 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1446 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1447 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1448 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1449 |     } else {
1450 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1451 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1452 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1453 |           next();
1454 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1455 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1456 |           next();
1457 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1458 |         }
1459 |       }
1460 | 
1461 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1462 |     }
1463 |   }

===== M0303 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 621-648
Documentation:

Method code:
621 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
622 |     Node lastJump = null;
623 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
624 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
625 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunction(curHandler)) {
626 |         break;
627 |       }
628 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
629 |         if (lastJump == null) {
630 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
631 |         } else {
632 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
633 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
634 |         }
635 |         lastJump = curHandler;
636 |       }
637 |     }
638 | 
639 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
640 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     if (lastJump == null) {
644 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
645 |     } else {
646 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
647 |     }
648 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 436-779
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
436 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
437 |     JSType childType;
438 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
439 |     Node left, right;
440 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
441 |     boolean typeable = true;
442 | 
443 |     switch (n.getType()) {
444 |       case Token.NAME:
445 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
446 |         break;
447 | 
448 |       case Token.LP:
449 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
450 |         // ignored here.
451 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
452 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
453 |         } else {
454 |           typeable = false;
455 |         }
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.COMMA:
459 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.TRUE:
463 |       case Token.FALSE:
464 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
465 |         break;
466 | 
467 |       case Token.THIS:
468 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
469 |         break;
470 | 
471 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
472 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
473 |         break;
474 | 
475 |       case Token.GET_REF:
476 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
477 |         break;
478 | 
479 |       case Token.NULL:
480 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.NUMBER:
484 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
485 |           ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
486 |         } else {
487 |           typeable = false;
488 |         }
489 |         break;
490 | 
491 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
492 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
493 |         break;
494 | 
495 |       case Token.STRING:
496 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
497 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
498 |         } else {
499 |           typeable = false;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 | 
503 |       case Token.REGEXP:
504 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
505 |         break;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.GETPROP:
508 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
509 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
510 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
511 |         break;
512 | 
513 |       case Token.GETELEM:
514 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
515 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
516 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
517 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
518 |         typeable = false;
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.VAR:
522 |         visitVar(t, n);
523 |         typeable = false;
524 |         break;
525 | 
526 |       case Token.NEW:
527 |         visitNew(t, n);
528 |         typeable = true;
529 |         break;
530 | 
531 |       case Token.CALL:
532 |         visitCall(t, n);
533 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
534 |         break;
535 | 
536 |       case Token.RETURN:
537 |         visitReturn(t, n);
538 |         typeable = false;
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.DEC:
542 |       case Token.INC:
543 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
544 |         validator.expectNumber(
545 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
546 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
547 |         break;
548 | 
549 |       case Token.NOT:
550 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
551 |         break;
552 | 
553 |       case Token.VOID:
554 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
555 |         break;
556 | 
557 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
558 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.BITNOT:
562 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
563 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
564 |           t.report(n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
565 |               childType.toString());
566 |         }
567 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
568 |         break;
569 | 
570 |       case Token.POS:
571 |       case Token.NEG:
572 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
573 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
574 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.EQ:
578 |       case Token.NE: {
579 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
580 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
581 | 
582 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
583 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
584 |         TernaryValue result =
585 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
586 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
587 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
588 |             result = result.not();
589 |           }
590 |           t.report(n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
591 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
592 |         }
593 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
594 |         break;
595 |       }
596 | 
597 |       case Token.SHEQ:
598 |       case Token.SHNE: {
599 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
600 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
601 | 
602 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
603 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
604 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
605 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
606 |           t.report(n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
607 |               rightType.toString());
608 |         }
609 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
610 |         break;
611 |       }
612 | 
613 |       case Token.LT:
614 |       case Token.LE:
615 |       case Token.GT:
616 |       case Token.GE:
617 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
618 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
619 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
620 |           validator.expectNumber(
621 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
622 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
623 |           validator.expectNumber(
624 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
625 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
626 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
627 |           // OK.
628 |         } else {
629 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
630 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
631 |           // should match a string context.
632 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
633 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
634 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
635 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
636 |           message = "right side of comparison";
637 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
638 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
639 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
640 |         }
641 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
642 |         break;
643 | 
644 |       case Token.IN:
645 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
646 |         right = n.getLastChild();
647 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
648 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
649 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
650 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
651 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
652 |         break;
653 | 
654 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
655 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
656 |         right = n.getLastChild();
657 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
658 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
659 | 
660 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
661 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
662 |         validator.expectActualObject(
663 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
664 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
665 |         break;
666 | 
667 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
668 |         visitAssign(t, n);
669 |         typeable = false;
670 |         break;
671 | 
672 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
673 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
674 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
675 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
676 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
677 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
678 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
679 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
680 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
681 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
682 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
683 |       case Token.LSH:
684 |       case Token.RSH:
685 |       case Token.URSH:
686 |       case Token.DIV:
687 |       case Token.MOD:
688 |       case Token.BITOR:
689 |       case Token.BITXOR:
690 |       case Token.BITAND:
691 |       case Token.SUB:
692 |       case Token.ADD:
693 |       case Token.MUL:
694 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
695 |         break;
696 | 
697 |       case Token.DELPROP:
698 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
699 |           t.report(n, BAD_DELETE);
700 |         }
701 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
702 |         break;
703 | 
704 |       case Token.CASE:
705 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
706 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
707 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
708 |         typeable = false;
709 |         break;
710 | 
711 |       case Token.WITH: {
712 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
713 |         childType = getJSType(child);
714 |         validator.expectObject(
715 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
716 |         typeable = false;
717 |         break;
718 |       }
719 | 
720 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
721 |         visitFunction(t, n);
722 |         break;
723 | 
724 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
725 |       case Token.LABEL:
726 |       case Token.SWITCH:
727 |       case Token.BREAK:
728 |       case Token.CATCH:
729 |       case Token.TRY:
730 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
731 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
732 |       case Token.BLOCK:
733 |       case Token.EMPTY:
734 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
735 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
736 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
737 |       case Token.THROW:
738 |         typeable = false;
739 |         break;
740 | 
741 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
742 |       case Token.DO:
743 |       case Token.FOR:
744 |       case Token.IF:
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |         typeable = false;
747 |         break;
748 | 
749 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
750 |       case Token.AND:
751 |       case Token.HOOK:
752 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
753 |       case Token.OR:
754 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
755 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
756 |         } else {
757 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
758 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
759 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
760 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
761 |           } else {
762 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
763 |           }
764 |         }
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       default:
768 |         t.report(n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
769 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
770 |         break;
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
774 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
775 | 
776 |     if (typeable) {
777 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
778 |     }
779 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
311 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
312 |     if (c <= 127) {
313 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
314 |     } else {
315 |       return c == 0xA0
316 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
317 |     }
318 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckUnreachableCode.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckUnreachableCode.java
Original range: 53-76
Documentation:

Method code:
53 |   @Override
54 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
55 |     GraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = t.getControlFlowGraph().getNode(n);
56 |     if (gNode != null && gNode.getAnnotation() != GraphReachability.REACHABLE) {
57 | 
58 |       // Only report error when there are some line number informations.
59 |       // There are synthetic nodes with no line number informations, nodes
60 |       // introduce by other passes (although not likely since this pass should
61 |       // be executed early) or some rhino bug.
62 |       if (n.getLineno() != -1 &&
63 |           // Allow spurious semi-colons and spurious breaks.
64 |           n.getType() != Token.EMPTY && n.getType() != Token.BREAK) {
65 |         compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, level, UNREACHABLE_CODE));
66 |         // From now on, we are going to assume the user fixed the error and not
67 |         // give more warning related to code section reachable from this node.
68 |         new GraphReachability<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
69 |             t.getControlFlowGraph()).recompute(n);
70 | 
71 |         // Saves time by not traversing children.
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 |     return true;
76 |   }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTypeNormalizer.normalizeJsDocAnnotations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTypeNormalizer.java
Original range: 68-97
Documentation:
/**
   * Normalize where JSDoc annotations appear on the AST.
   *
   * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
   * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
   * value. For example,
   * <pre>
   * var x = {
   *   / JSDOC /
   *   a: 'b',
   *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
   * };
   * </pre>
   *
   * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
   * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
   */
Method code:
68 |   private void normalizeJsDocAnnotations(Node n) {
69 |     if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
70 |       for (Node key = n.getFirstChild();
71 |            key != null; key = key.getNext().getNext()) {
72 |         Node value = key.getNext();
73 |         if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
74 |             key.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
75 |           value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
76 |         }
77 |       }
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Determine if it is possible to simply use the javadoc
81 |     // everywhere rather than use IS_DISPATCHER.
82 |     /*
83 |      * Translate dispatcher info into the property expected node.
84 |      */
85 |     if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isJavaDispatch()) {
86 |       if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
87 |         Node fnNode = n.getLastChild();
88 |         Preconditions.checkState(fnNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
89 |         fnNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_DISPATCHER, true);
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
94 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
95 |       normalizeJsDocAnnotations(child);
96 |     }
97 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 177-276
Documentation:

Method code:
177 |   @Override
178 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
179 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
180 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
181 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
182 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
183 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
184 |     // worthwhile.
185 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
186 |     Node condition = null;
187 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
188 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
189 | 
190 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
191 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
192 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
193 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
194 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
195 | 
196 |       switch (branch) {
197 |         case ON_TRUE:
198 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
199 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
200 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
201 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
202 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
203 |             }
204 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
205 |               FlowScope informed = output.createChildFlowScope();
206 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(),
207 |                   getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
208 |               newScope = informed;
209 |             }
210 |             break;
211 |           }
212 | 
213 |           // FALL THROUGH
214 | 
215 |         case ON_FALSE:
216 |           if (condition == null) {
217 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
218 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
219 |               condition = source;
220 | 
221 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
222 |               // of the loop.
223 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
224 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
225 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
226 |               }
227 |             }
228 |           }
229 | 
230 |           if (condition != null) {
231 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
232 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
233 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
234 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
235 |               // scope on false.
236 |               //
237 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
238 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
239 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
240 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
241 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
242 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
243 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
244 | 
245 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
246 |               // of the loop.
247 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
248 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
249 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
250 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
251 |               }
252 |               newScope =
253 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
254 |                       condition,
255 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
256 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
257 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
258 |             } else {
259 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
260 |               // of the loop.
261 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
262 |                 conditionFlowScope =
263 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
264 |               }
265 |               newScope =
266 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
267 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
268 |             }
269 |           }
270 |           break;
271 |       }
272 | 
273 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
274 |     }
275 |     return result;
276 |   }

281 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
282 |     switch (c) {
283 |       case '@':
284 |       case '*':

286 |       case '>':
287 |       case ':':

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 60-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
60 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
61 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
62 |     int c;
63 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
64 |     for (;;) {
65 |       // eat white spaces
66 |       for (;;) {
67 |         charno = -1;
68 |         c = getChar();
69 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
71 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
72 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
73 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
74 |           break;
75 |         }
76 |       }
77 | 
78 |       switch (c) {
79 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
80 |         case '@':
81 |           do {
82 |             c = getChar();
83 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
84 |               addToString(c);
85 |             } else {
86 |               ungetChar(c);
87 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
88 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
89 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
90 |             }
91 |           } while (true);
92 | 
93 |         case '*':
94 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
95 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
96 |           } else {
97 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
98 |           }
99 | 
100 |         case ',':
101 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
102 | 
103 |         case '>':
104 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
105 | 
106 |         case '(':
107 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
108 | 
109 |         case ')':
110 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
111 | 
112 |         case '{':
113 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
114 | 
115 |         case '}':
116 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
117 | 
118 |         case '[':
119 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
120 | 
121 |         case ']':
122 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
123 | 
124 |         case '?':
125 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
126 | 
127 |         case '!':
128 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
129 | 
130 |         case ':':
131 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
132 | 
133 |         case '=':
134 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
135 | 
136 |         case '|':
137 |           matchChar('|');
138 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
139 | 
140 |         case '.':
141 |           c = getChar();
142 |           if (c == '<') {
143 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
144 |           } else {
145 |             if (c == '.') {
146 |               c = getChar();
147 |               if (c == '.') {
148 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
149 |               } else {
150 |                 addToString('.');
151 |               }
152 |             }
153 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
154 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
155 |             c = '.';
156 |           }
157 |           // fall through
158 | 
159 |         default: {
160 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
161 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
162 |           int c1 = c;
163 |           addToString(c);
164 |           int c2 = getChar();
165 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
166 |             ungetChar(c2);
167 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
168 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
169 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
170 |           } else {
171 |             do {
172 |               c1 = c2;
173 |               c2 = getChar();
174 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
175 |                 ungetChar(c2);
176 |                 ungetChar(c1);
177 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
178 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
179 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
180 |               } else {
181 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                 } else {
184 |                   ungetChar(c2);
185 |                   addToString(c1);
186 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
187 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
188 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
189 |                 }
190 |               }
191 |             } while (true);
192 |           }
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0350 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 670-763
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
670 |   private Node computeFollowNode(Node fromNode, Node node) {
671 |     /*
672 |      * This is the case where:
673 |      *
674 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
675 |      * the script.
676 |      *
677 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
678 |      * the caller of the function.
679 |      *
680 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
681 |      * back to the caller of the function.
682 |      *
683 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
684 |      * asked to traverse.
685 |      *
686 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
687 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
688 |      */
689 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
690 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION || node == root) {
691 |       return null;
692 |     }
693 | 
694 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
695 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
696 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
697 |       case Token.IF:
698 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
699 |       case Token.CASE:
700 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
701 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
702 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
703 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
704 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
705 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
706 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
707 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
708 |           } else {
709 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
710 |           }
711 |         } else {
712 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
713 |         }
714 |         break;
715 |       case Token.FOR:
716 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
717 |           return parent;
718 |         } else {
719 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
720 |         }
721 |       case Token.WHILE:
722 |       case Token.DO:
723 |         return parent;
724 |       case Token.TRY:
725 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
726 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
727 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
728 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
729 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
730 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
731 |           }
732 |         // CATCH block.
733 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
734 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
735 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
736 |           } else {
737 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
738 |           }
739 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
740 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
741 |           for (Node finallyNode : finallyMap.get(parent)) {
742 |             createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
743 |           }
744 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
745 |         }
746 |     }
747 | 
748 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
749 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
750 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
751 | 
752 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
753 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
754 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
758 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
759 |     } else {
760 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
761 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
762 |     }
763 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 512-548
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
512 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
513 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
514 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
515 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
516 | 
517 |     if (existingType != null) {
518 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
519 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
520 |         FunctionType existingFn =
521 |             isInstanceObject ?
522 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
523 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
524 | 
525 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
526 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
527 |         }
528 | 
529 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
530 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
531 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
532 |         }
533 | 
534 |         return existingFn;
535 |       } else {
536 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
537 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
538 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
539 |       }
540 |     }
541 | 
542 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
543 | 
544 |     if (scope.isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
545 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
546 |     }
547 |     return fnType;
548 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 483-516
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
483 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
484 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
485 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
486 | 
487 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
488 | 
489 |     boolean anonymous = parent != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n);
490 | 
491 |     if (!anonymous) {
492 |       // Named functions are parent of the containing scope.
493 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     curNode = n;
497 |     pushScope(n);
498 | 
499 |     if (anonymous) {
500 |       // Anonymous function names are parent of the contained scope.
501 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
505 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
506 | 
507 |     // Args
508 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
509 | 
510 |     // Body
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
512 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
513 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
514 | 
515 |     popScope();
516 |   }

===== M0370 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

===== M0376 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 889-909
Documentation:

Method code:
889 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
890 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
891 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
892 |       return s;
893 |     }
894 | 
895 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
896 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
897 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
898 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
899 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
900 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
901 |       // crazy.
902 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
903 |         sb.append(c);
904 |       } else {
905 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
906 |       }
907 |     }
908 |     return sb.toString();
909 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
251 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
252 |     this.type = type;
253 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
254 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
255 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
256 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
257 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
258 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
259 |       int i = 0;
260 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
261 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
262 |         i++;
263 |       }
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
267 | 
268 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
269 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
270 |       return;

272 |

274 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
275 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
276 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
277 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
278 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
279 |       trace.truncateEvents();
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
283 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
284 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
285 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
286 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
287 |                     + "try/finally block. "
288 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
289 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
290 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     trace.startEvent(this);
294 |   }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 519-539
Documentation:

Method code:
519 |     @Override
520 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
521 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
522 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
523 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
524 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());
525 | 
526 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> predNode : predNodes) {
527 |         BranchedFlowState<L> predNodeState = predNode.getAnnotation();
528 | 
529 |         L in = predNodeState.out.get(
530 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
531 | 
532 |         values.add(in);
533 |       }
534 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
535 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
536 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
537 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
538 |       }
539 |     }

===== M0415 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineName(Node,Node,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 412-441
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a variable based on the {@link Token#NAME} node passed.
     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.
     * @param var The parent of the {@code name} node, which must be a
     *     {@link Token#VAR} node.
     * @param parent {@code var}'s parent.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} information relating to this
     *     {@code name} node.
     */
Method code:
412 |     private void defineName(Node name, Node var, Node parent, JSDocInfo info) {
413 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
414 | 
415 |       if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
416 |         // function
417 |         String functionName = name.getString();
418 |         FunctionType functionType =
419 |             getFunctionType(functionName, value, info, null);
420 |         defineSlot(name, var, functionType);
421 |       } else {
422 |         // variable's type
423 |         JSType type = null;
424 |         if (info == null) {
425 |           // the variable's type will be inferred
426 |           CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
427 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(input, sourceName);
428 |           type = input.isExtern() ?
429 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : null;
430 |         } else if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
431 |           type = getEnumType(name.getString(), var, value,
432 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope));
433 |         } else if (info.isConstructor()) {
434 |           type = getFunctionType(name.getString(), value, info, name);
435 |         } else {
436 |           type = getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, name, info);
437 |         }
438 | 
439 |         defineSlot(name, var, type);
440 |       }
441 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
454 |     @Override
455 |     void maybeCutLine() {
456 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
457 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
458 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
459 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
460 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
461 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
462 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
463 |           lineIndex++;
464 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
465 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
466 |         } else {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * Visit a node in a local scope, and add any local variables or catch
     * parameters into the local symbol table.
     *
     * @param t The node traversal.
     * @param n The node being visited.
     * @param parent The parent of n
     */
Method code:
1264 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1265 |       if (n == scope.getRootNode()) return;
1266 | 
1267 |       if (n.getType() == Token.LP && parent == scope.getRootNode()) {
1268 |         handleFunctionInputs(parent);
1269 |         return;
1270 |       }
1271 | 
1272 |       switch (n.getType()) {
1273 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
1274 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled separately.
1275 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
1276 |             return;
1277 |           }
1278 |           define(n, parent);
1279 |           break;
1280 | 
1281 |         case Token.CATCH:
1282 |         case Token.VAR:
1283 |           define(n, parent);
1284 |           break;
1285 |       }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CreateSyntheticBlocks.Callback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CreateSyntheticBlocks.java
Original range: 95-158
Documentation:

Method code:
95 |     @Override
96 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
97 |       if (n.getType() != Token.NAME || parent.getType() != Token.CALL) {
98 |         return;
99 |       }
100 | 
101 |       if (startMarkerName.equals(n.getString())) {
102 |         // Record information needed for insertion of a synthetic block or
103 |         // warning about an unmatched start marker.
104 |         Node ancestorBlockChild = n;
105 |         Node ancestorBlock = null;
106 |         for (Node ancestor : n.getAncestors()) {
107 |           int type = ancestor.getType();
108 |           if (type == Token.SCRIPT || type == Token.BLOCK) {
109 |             ancestorBlock = ancestor;
110 |             break;
111 |           }
112 |           ancestorBlockChild = ancestor;
113 |         }
114 | 
115 |         startMarkerStack.push(new StartMarker(t.getSourceName(), n,
116 |             ancestorBlockChild, ancestorBlock));
117 |       }
118 | 
119 |       if (!endMarkerName.equals(n.getString())) {
120 |         return;
121 |       }
122 | 
123 |       if (startMarkerStack.isEmpty()) {
124 |         compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, UNMATCHED_END_MARKER,
125 |             startMarkerName, endMarkerName));
126 |         return;
127 |       }
128 | 
129 |       StartMarker startMarker = startMarkerStack.pop();
130 | 
131 |       Node endMarkerAncestorBlockChild = n;
132 |       for (Node ancestor : n.getAncestors()) {
133 |         int type = ancestor.getType();
134 |         if (type == Token.SCRIPT || type == Token.BLOCK) {
135 |           if (ancestor != startMarker.ancestorBlock) {
136 |             // The end marker isn't in the same block as the start marker.
137 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, UNMATCHED_END_MARKER,
138 |                 startMarkerName, endMarkerName));
139 |             return;
140 |           }
141 |           break;
142 |         }
143 |         endMarkerAncestorBlockChild = ancestor;
144 |       }
145 | 
146 |       Node block = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
147 |       block.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
148 |       startMarker.ancestorBlock.addChildAfter(block,
149 |           startMarker.ancestorBlockChild);
150 |       Node removedNode = null;
151 |       do {
152 |         // Move the nodes into the synthetic block.
153 |         removedNode = startMarker.ancestorBlock.removeChildAfter(block);
154 |         block.addChildToBack(removedNode);
155 |       } while (removedNode != endMarkerAncestorBlockChild);
156 | 
157 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
158 |     }

189 |   @Override
190 |   public int hashCode() {
191 |     int hashCode = 0;
192 |     if (returnType != null) {
193 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
194 |     }
195 |     if (parameters != null) {
196 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
197 |       while (param != null) {
198 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
199 |         if (paramType != null) {
200 |           hashCode += paramType.hashCode();
201 |         }
202 |         param = param.getNext();
203 |       }
204 |     }
205 |     return hashCode;
206 |   }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 360-404
Documentation:

Method code:
360 |   @Override
361 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
362 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
363 |       return true;
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     // Union types
367 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
368 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
369 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
370 |       return false;
371 |     }
372 | 
373 |     // record types
374 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
375 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
376 |     }
377 | 
378 |     // Interfaces
379 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
380 |     // to the interface instance.
381 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
382 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
383 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
384 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
385 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
386 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
387 |           return true;
388 |         }
389 |       }
390 |     }
391 | 
392 |     // other prototype based objects
393 |     if (that != null) {
394 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
395 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
396 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
397 |         // to avoid guessing.
398 |         return true;
399 |       }
400 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     return false;
404 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken,WhitespaceOption)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1113-1202
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1113 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1114 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1115 | 
1116 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1117 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1118 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1119 |     }
1120 | 
1121 |     stream.update();
1122 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1123 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1124 | 
1125 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1126 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1127 | 
1128 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1129 |       line = line.trim();
1130 |     }
1131 | 
1132 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1133 |     builder.append(line);
1134 | 
1135 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1136 |     token = next();
1137 | 
1138 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1139 | 
1140 |     do {
1141 |       switch (token) {
1142 |         case ANNOTATION:
1143 |         case EOC:
1144 |         case EOF:
1145 |           String multilineText = builder.toString();
1146 | 
1147 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1148 |             multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1149 |           }
1150 | 
1151 |           int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1152 |           int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1153 | 
1154 |           if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1155 |             jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1156 |                                   endLineno, endCharno);
1157 |           }
1158 | 
1159 |           return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1160 | 
1161 |         case STAR:
1162 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1163 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1164 |               builder.append(' ');
1165 |             }
1166 | 
1167 |             builder.append('*');
1168 |           }
1169 | 
1170 |           token = next();
1171 |           continue;
1172 | 
1173 |         case EOL:
1174 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1175 |             builder.append("\n");
1176 |           }
1177 | 
1178 |           ignoreStar = true;
1179 |           token = next();
1180 |           continue;
1181 | 
1182 |         default:
1183 |           ignoreStar = false;
1184 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1185 | 
1186 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1187 |             builder.append(' ');
1188 |           }
1189 | 
1190 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1191 | 
1192 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1193 | 
1194 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1195 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1196 |           }
1197 | 
1198 |           builder.append(line);
1199 |           token = next();
1200 |       }
1201 |     } while (true);
1202 |   }

740 |         PropListItem x = propListHead;

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getFunctionType(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 454-548
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the function type from the function node and its attached
     * {@link JSDocInfo}.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param rValue the function node. It must be a {@link Token#FUNCTION}.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
454 |     private FunctionType getFunctionType(String name,
455 |         Node rValue, JSDocInfo info, @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
456 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
457 | 
458 |       // Handle function aliases.
459 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName()) {
460 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
461 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
462 |           functionType = (FunctionType) var.getType();
463 |           if (functionType != null && functionType.isConstructor()) {
464 |             typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
465 |           }
466 |         }
467 |         return functionType;
468 |       }
469 | 
470 |       Node owner = null;
471 |       if (lvalueNode != null) {
472 |         owner = getPrototypePropertyOwner(lvalueNode);
473 |       }
474 | 
475 |       Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
476 |       boolean isFnLiteral =
477 |           rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
478 |       Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
479 |       Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
480 |           rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
481 | 
482 |       if (functionType == null && info != null && info.hasType()) {
483 |         JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope);
484 | 
485 |         // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
486 |         type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
487 |         if (type.isFunctionType()) {
488 |           functionType = (FunctionType) type;
489 |           functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
490 |         }
491 |       }
492 | 
493 |       if (functionType == null) {
494 |         if (info == null ||
495 |             !FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
496 |           // We don't really have any type information in the annotation.
497 |           // Before we give up on this function, look at the object we're
498 |           // assigning it to. For example, if the function looks like this:
499 |           // SubFoo.prototype.bar = function() { ... };
500 |           // We can use type information on Foo.prototype.bar and apply it
501 |           // to this function.
502 |           if (lvalueNode != null && lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
503 |               lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
504 |             Var var = scope.getVar(
505 |                 lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
506 |             if (var != null) {
507 |               ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
508 |               FunctionType propType = null;
509 |               if (ownerType != null) {
510 |                 propType = findOverriddenFunction(
511 |                     ownerType, lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString());
512 |               }
513 | 
514 |               if (propType != null) {
515 |                 functionType =
516 |                     new FunctionTypeBuilder(
517 |                         name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName, scope)
518 |                     .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
519 |                     .inferFromOverriddenFunction(propType, parametersNode)
520 |                     .inferThisType(info, owner)
521 |                     .buildAndRegister();
522 |               }
523 |             }
524 |           }
525 |         }
526 |       } // end if (functionType == null)
527 | 
528 |       if (functionType == null) {
529 |         functionType =
530 |             new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
531 |                 scope)
532 |             .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
533 |             .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
534 |             .inferReturnType(info)
535 |             .inferInheritance(info)
536 |             .inferThisType(info, owner)
537 |             .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
538 |             .buildAndRegister();
539 |       }
540 | 
541 |       // assigning the function type to the function node
542 |       if (rValue != null) {
543 |         rValue.setJSType(functionType);
544 |       }
545 | 
546 |       // all done
547 |       return functionType;
548 |     }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1673-1697
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1673 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1674 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1675 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1676 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1677 |             type.isUnknownType());
1678 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1679 |     if (info != null) {
1680 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1681 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope());
1682 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1683 |         type = infoType;
1684 |       }
1685 | 
1686 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1687 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1688 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1689 |         compiler.report(
1690 |             JSError.make(t, n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1691 |       }
1692 |     }
1693 | 
1694 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1695 |       n.setJSType(type);
1696 |     }
1697 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 510-562
Documentation:

Method code:
510 |    @Override
511 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
512 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
513 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
514 |       if (name == null) {
515 |         name = new Name();
516 |         name.setIdentifier("");
517 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
518 |       }
519 |       Node node = new com.google.javascript.rhino.FunctionNode(
520 |           name.getIdentifier());
521 |       node.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, functionNode.getSourceName());
522 |       Node newName = transform(name);
523 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
524 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
525 |         // declaration.
526 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
527 |         // TODO(user) Mark line number of paren correctly.
528 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
529 |         // same line as the function keyword.
530 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
531 |             functionNode.getLp();
532 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
536 |       Node lp = new Node(Token.LP);
537 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
538 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
539 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
540 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
541 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
542 |       // TODO(user): Mark line number of paren correctly.
543 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
544 |       if (fnName != null) {
545 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
546 |       } else {
547 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
548 |       }
549 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
550 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
551 | 
552 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
553 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
554 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
555 |       }
556 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
557 | 
558 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
559 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
560 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
561 |      return node;
562 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1138-1168
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1138 |     private void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1139 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1140 |         Node n = stub.node;
1141 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1142 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1143 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1144 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1145 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1146 | 
1147 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1148 |           continue;
1149 |         }
1150 | 
1151 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1152 |         // in the type registry.
1153 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1154 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1155 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1156 | 
1157 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1158 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1159 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1160 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1161 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1162 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern);
1163 |         } else {
1164 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1165 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1166 |         }
1167 |       }
1168 |     }

   * properties is unknown.

403 |   @Override
404 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
405 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
406 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1469-1494
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1469 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1470 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1471 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1472 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1473 |       skipEOLs();
1474 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1475 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1476 |       skipEOLs();
1477 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1478 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1479 |       skipEOLs();
1480 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1481 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1482 |       String string = stream.getString();
1483 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1484 |         skipEOLs();
1485 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1486 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1487 |         return newStringNode(string);
1488 |       } else {
1489 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1490 |       }
1491 |     }
1492 | 
1493 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1494 |   }

/**
   * If e is already in a non-trivial equivalence class, that is, a class with
   * more than two elements, then return the {@link Node} corresponding to the
   * representative element. Otherwise, if e sits in an equivalence class by
   * itself, then create a {@link Node}, put it into elmap and return it.

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 806-833
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
806 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
807 |     // unknown
808 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
809 |       return true;
810 |     }
811 |     // equality
812 |     if (thisType.equals(thatType)) {
813 |       return true;
814 |     }
815 |     // all type
816 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
817 |       return true;
818 |     }
819 |     // unions
820 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
821 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
822 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
823 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
824 |           return true;
825 |         }
826 |       }
827 |     }
828 |     // named types
829 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
830 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
831 |     }
832 |     return false;
833 |   }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.StandardUnionFind.union(E,E)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/StandardUnionFind.java
Original range: 81-99
Documentation:

Method code:
81 |   public E union(E a, E b) {
82 |     Node<E> nodeA = findRootOrCreateNode(a);
83 |     Node<E> nodeB = findRootOrCreateNode(b);
84 | 
85 |     if (nodeA == nodeB) {
86 |       return nodeA.element;
87 |     }
88 |     if (nodeA.rank > nodeB.rank) {
89 |       nodeB.parent = nodeA;
90 |       nodeA.size += nodeB.size;
91 |       return nodeA.element;
92 |     }
93 |     nodeA.parent = nodeB;
94 |     if (nodeA.rank == nodeB.rank) {
95 |       nodeB.rank++;
96 |     }
97 |     nodeB.size += nodeA.size;
98 |     return nodeB.element;
99 |   }

/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
202 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
203 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
204 |     int c;
205 |     for (;;) {
206 |       c = getChar();

209 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {

211 |             break;

220 |

223 |           break;
224 |       }

Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is testing for the existence of a property.
   * If true, we will not emit warnings about a missing property.
   *
   * @param getProp The GETPROP being tested.
   */
Method code:
1204 |   private boolean isPropertyTest(Node getProp) {
1205 |     Node parent = getProp.getParent();
1206 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1207 |       case Token.CALL:
1208 |         return parent.getFirstChild() != getProp &&
1209 |             compiler.getCodingConvention().isPropertyTestFunction(parent);
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.IF:
1212 |       case Token.WHILE:

1215 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) == getProp;
1216 | 
1217 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1218 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1219 |         return true;
1220 | 
1221 |       case Token.AND:
1222 |       case Token.HOOK:
1223 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == getProp;
1224 |     }
1225 |     return false;
1226 |   }

===== M0546 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 85-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
85 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
86 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
87 |     // just bail out early here.
88 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
89 |       return this;
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
93 | 
94 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
95 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
96 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
97 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
98 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
99 |     }
100 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
101 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
102 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
103 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
104 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
105 |         }
106 |       } else {
107 |         if (!alternate.isUnknownType()) {
108 |           Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
109 |           while (it.hasNext()) {
110 |             JSType current = it.next();
111 |             if (!current.isUnknownType()) {
112 |               if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
113 |                 // Alternate is unnecessary.
114 |                 return this;
115 |               } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
116 |                 // Alternate makes current obsolete
117 |                 it.remove();
118 |               }
119 |             }
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |         alternates.add(alternate);
123 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
124 |       }
125 |     } else {
126 |       result = null;
127 |     }
128 |     return this;
129 |   }
