Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodes(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1167-1295
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a JSType from the nodes representing a type.
   * @param n The node with type info.
   * @param sourceName The source file name.
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name lookups.
   */
Method code:
1167 |   public JSType createFromTypeNodes(Node n, String sourceName,
1168 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1169 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1170 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1171 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1172 | 
1173 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1174 |         return createFromTypeNodes(n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1175 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1176 | 
1177 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable
1178 |         return createNullableType(
1179 |             createFromTypeNodes(n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1180 | 
1181 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1182 |         return createOptionalType(
1183 |             createFromTypeNodes(n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1184 | 
1185 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1186 |         return createOptionalType(
1187 |             createFromTypeNodes(n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1188 | 
1189 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1190 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1191 | 
1192 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1193 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1194 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1195 | 
1196 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1197 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1198 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1199 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1200 |           builder.addAlternate(createFromTypeNodes(child, sourceName, scope));
1201 |         }
1202 |         return builder.build();
1203 | 
1204 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1205 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1206 | 
1207 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1208 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1209 | 
1210 |       case Token.STRING:
1211 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1212 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1213 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1214 |             !(enumTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1215 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1216 |           if (typeList != null &&
1217 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1218 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1219 |             JSType parameterType =
1220 |                 createFromTypeNodes(
1221 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1222 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1223 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1224 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1225 |               JSType indexType =
1226 |                   createFromTypeNodes(
1227 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1228 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1229 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1230 |             }
1231 |           }
1232 |           return createNullableType(namedType);
1233 | 
1234 |         } else {
1235 |           return namedType;
1236 |         }
1237 | 
1238 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1239 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1240 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1241 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS) {
1242 |           Node thisNode = current.getFirstChild();
1243 |           thisType =
1244 |               ObjectType.cast(
1245 |                   createFromTypeNodes(thisNode, sourceName, scope)
1246 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1247 |           if (thisType == null) {
1248 |             reporter.warning(
1249 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject"),
1250 |                 sourceName, thisNode.getLineno(), "", thisNode.getCharno());
1251 |           }
1252 | 
1253 |           current = current.getNext();
1254 |         }
1255 | 
1256 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1257 | 
1258 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1259 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1260 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1261 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1262 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1263 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1264 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1265 |               } else {
1266 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1267 |                     createFromTypeNodes(
1268 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1269 |               }
1270 |             } else {
1271 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodes(arg, sourceName, scope);
1272 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1273 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1274 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1275 |                   reporter.warning(
1276 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1277 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1278 |                 }
1279 |               } else {
1280 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1281 |               }
1282 |             }
1283 |           }
1284 |           current = current.getNext();
1285 |         }
1286 | 
1287 |         JSType returnType = createFromTypeNodes(current, sourceName, scope);
1288 | 
1289 |         return new FunctionType(this, null, null, paramBuilder.build(),
1290 |              returnType, thisType, null);
1291 |     }
1292 | 
1293 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1294 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1295 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1217 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1218 | 
1219 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1220 | 
1221 |     do {
1222 |       switch (token) {
1223 |         case ANNOTATION:
1224 |         case EOC:
1225 |         case EOF:
1226 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1227 | 
1228 |         case STAR:
1229 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1230 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1231 |               builder.append(' ');
1232 |             }
1233 | 
1234 |             builder.append('*');
1235 |           }
1236 | 
1237 |           token = next();
1238 |           continue;
1239 | 
1240 |         case EOL:
1241 |           ignoreStar = true;
1242 |           builder.append('\n');
1243 |           token = next();
1244 |           continue;
1245 | 
1246 |         default:
1247 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1248 |             builder.append(' ');
1249 |           }
1250 | 
1251 |           ignoreStar = false;
1252 | 
1253 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1254 | 
1255 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1256 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1257 |           builder.append(line);
1258 |           token = next();
1259 |       }
1260 |     } while (true);
1261 |   }

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 192-211
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
192 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
193 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
194 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
195 |     worklist.add(entry);
196 | 
197 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
198 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
199 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
200 |         continue;
201 |       }
202 | 
203 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
204 | 
205 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
206 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
207 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
208 |         worklist.add(candidate);
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 |   }

/**
   * Declares a property on its owner, if necessary.
   * @return True if a property was declared.
   */
Method code:
600 |   private boolean ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(
601 |       Node getprop, ObjectType objectType) {
602 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
603 |     String qName = getprop.getQualifiedName();
604 |     if (qName != null) {
605 |       Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(qName);
606 |       if (var != null && !var.isTypeInferred()) {
607 |         // Handle normal declarations that could not be addressed earlier.

611 |              (!objectType.isInstanceType() ||
612 |                  (var.isExtern() && !objectType.isNativeObjectType())))) {

===== M0595 =====

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args

103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 327-376
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
327 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
328 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
329 |     if (argsParent == null) {
330 |       if (info == null) {
331 |         return this;
332 |       } else {
333 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
334 |       }
335 |     }
336 | 
337 |     // arguments
338 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
339 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
340 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
341 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
342 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
343 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
344 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
345 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
346 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
347 | 
348 |       // type from JSDocInfo
349 |       JSType parameterType =
350 |           info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName) ?
351 |           info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope) :
352 |           typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
353 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
354 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
355 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
356 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
357 |         }
358 |         foundTemplateType = true;
359 |       }
360 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
361 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
362 |           isOptionalParameter(arg, info),
363 |           isVarArgsParameter(arg, info));
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
367 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
371 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
372 |     }
373 | 
374 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
375 |     return this;
376 |   }

===== M0610 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 986-1005
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
986 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
987 |     switch (n.getType()) {
988 |       case Token.IF:
989 |       case Token.WHILE:
990 |         return n.getFirstChild();
991 |       case Token.DO:
992 |         return n.getLastChild();
993 |       case Token.FOR:
994 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
995 |           case 3:
996 |             return null;
997 |           case 4:
998 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
999 |         }
1000 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1001 |       case Token.CASE:
1002 |         return null;
1003 |     }
1004 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1005 |   }

===== M0622 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1174-1196
Documentation:
/**
   * Make sure that the access of this property is ok.
   */
Method code:
1174 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1175 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1176 |     ObjectType objectType = childType.dereference();
1177 |     if (objectType != null) {
1178 |       JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1179 |       if ((!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1180 |            objectType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) &&
1181 |           propType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1182 |         if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1183 |           t.report(n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1184 |         } else if (!objectType.isEmptyType() &&
1185 |             reportMissingProperties && !isPropertyTest(n)) {
1186 |           if (!typeRegistry.canPropertyBeDefined(objectType, propName)) {
1187 |             t.report(n, INEXISTENT_PROPERTY, propName,
1188 |                 validator.getReadableJSTypeName(n.getFirstChild(), true));
1189 |           }
1190 |         }
1191 |       }
1192 |     } else {
1193 |       // TODO(nicksantos): might want to flag the access on a non object when
1194 |       // it's impossible to get a property from this type.
1195 |     }
1196 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
139 |   boolean parse() {
140 |     int lineno;
141 |     int charno;
142 | 
143 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
144 |     JSTypeExpression type;
145 | 
146 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
147 |     JsDocToken token = next();
148 | 
149 |     ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
150 |     token = blockInfo.token;
151 | 
152 |     // If we have a block level comment, record it.
153 |     if (blockInfo.string.length() > 0) {
154 |       jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
158 |     retry: for (;;) {
159 |       switch (token) {
160 |         case ANNOTATION:
161 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
162 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
163 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
164 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
165 | 
166 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
167 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
168 |             if (annotation == null) {
169 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
170 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
171 |             } else {
172 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
173 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
174 | 
175 |               switch (annotation) {
176 |                 case AUTHOR:
177 |                   ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
178 |                   String author = authorInfo.string;
179 | 
180 |                   if (author.length() == 0) {
181 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
182 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
183 |                   } else {
184 |                     jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
185 |                   }
186 | 
187 |                   token = authorInfo.token;
188 |                   continue retry;
189 | 
190 |                 case CONSTANT:
191 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
192 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
193 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
194 |                   }
195 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
196 |                   continue retry;
197 | 
198 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
199 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
200 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
201 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
202 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
203 |                     } else {
204 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
205 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
206 |                     }
207 |                   }
208 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
209 |                   continue retry;
210 | 
211 |                 case DEPRECATED:
212 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
213 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
214 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
215 |                   }
216 | 
217 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
218 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
219 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
220 | 
221 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
222 | 
223 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
224 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
225 |                   }

227 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
228 |                   continue retry;

232 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
233 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
234 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
235 |                     } else {

237 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                     }
239 |                   }

241 |                   continue retry;

245 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
246 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
247 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
248 |                     continue retry;
249 |                   } else {
250 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
251 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
252 | 
253 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
254 | 
255 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
256 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
257 |                     continue retry;
258 |                   }

261 |                   ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
262 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
263 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.TRIM);

695 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define.badtype",
696 |                               lineno, charno);
697 |                         } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
698 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
699 |                               lineno, charno);
700 |                         }
701 |                         break;
702 | 
703 |                       case RETURN:
704 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
705 |                           parser.addWarning(
706 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
707 |                           break;
708 |                         }
709 | 
710 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
711 |                         token = current();
712 | 
713 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
714 |                         ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
715 |                             extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
716 | 
717 |                         String returnDescription = returnDescriptionInfo.string;
718 | 
719 |                         if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
720 |                           jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
721 |                               returnDescription);
722 |                         }
723 | 
724 |                         token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
725 |                         continue retry;
726 | 
727 |                       case THIS:
728 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
729 |                           parser.addWarning(
730 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
731 |                         }
732 |                         break;
733 | 
734 |                       case TYPE:
735 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
736 |                           parser.addWarning(
737 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
738 |                         }
739 |                         break;
740 | 
741 |                       case TYPEDEF:
742 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
743 |                           parser.addWarning(
744 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
745 |                         }
746 |                         break;
747 |                     }
748 | 
749 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
750 |                   }
751 |                   continue retry;
752 |               }
753 |             }
754 |           }
755 |           break;
756 | 
757 |         case EOC:
758 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
759 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
760 |           }
761 | 
762 |           return true;
763 | 
764 |         case EOF:
765 |           // discard any accumulated information
766 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
767 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
768 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
769 |           return false;
770 | 
771 |         case EOL:
772 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
773 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
774 |           }
775 |           token = next();
776 |           continue retry;
777 | 
778 |         default:
779 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
780 |             token = next();
781 |             continue retry;
782 |           } else {
783 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
784 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
785 |             continue retry;
786 |           }
787 |       }
788 | 
789 |       // next token
790 |       token = next();
791 |     }
792 |   }

93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
100 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
101 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
102 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
114 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
115 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else {
118 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
119 |       }
120 |       return;
121 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,JSType,boolean)

/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
657 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
658 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
659 | 
660 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualfied names.
661 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = false;
662 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
663 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
664 |             parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
665 |             parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
666 |             parent.getType() == Token.LP ||
667 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH);
668 |         shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = scope.isGlobal() &&
669 |             (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
670 |              parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
671 |       } else {
672 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
673 |             n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
674 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
675 |              parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT));
676 |       }
677 |       String variableName = n.getQualifiedName();
678 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
679 | 
680 |       // declared in closest scope?
681 |       if (scope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
682 |         Var oldVar = scope.getVar(variableName);
683 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
684 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
685 |       } else {
686 |         if (!inferred) {
687 |           n.setJSType(type);
688 |         }
689 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
690 |         scope.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
691 | 
692 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
693 |           ObjectType globalThis =
694 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
695 |           boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
696 |           if (inferred) {
697 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
698 |                 type == null ?
699 |                     typeRegistry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
700 |                     type,
701 |                 isExtern);
702 |           } else {
703 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern);
704 |           }
705 |         }
706 | 
707 |         // If we're in the global scope, also declare var.prototype
708 |         // in the scope chain.
709 |         if (scope.isGlobal() && type instanceof FunctionType) {
710 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
711 |           if (fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) {
712 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
713 |             scope.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
714 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), compiler.getInput(sourceName),

===== M0630 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1331-1366
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1331 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1332 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1333 | 
1334 |     skipEOLs();
1335 | 
1336 |     boolean restArg = false;
1337 |     token = next();
1338 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1339 |       token = next();
1340 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1341 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1342 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1343 |       }
1344 |       restArg = true;
1345 |     }
1346 | 
1347 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1348 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1349 |       skipEOLs();
1350 |       if (restArg) {
1351 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1352 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1353 |         next();
1354 |         skipEOLs();
1355 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1356 |       }
1357 | 
1358 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1359 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1360 |       } else {
1361 |         next();
1362 |       }
1363 |     }
1364 | 
1365 |     return typeNode;
1366 |   }

===== M0642 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 994-1094
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
994 |     private void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
995 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
996 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
997 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
998 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
999 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1000 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1001 | 
1002 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1003 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1004 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1005 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1006 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1007 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1008 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1009 |         if (qVar != null) {
1010 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1011 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1012 |             return;
1013 |           }
1014 |           scope.undeclare(qVar);
1015 |         }
1016 |       }
1017 | 
1018 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1019 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1020 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1021 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1022 |       // 4) ASSIGN to anything else
1023 |       //
1024 |       // 1 and 3 are declarations, 4 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1025 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1026 |       //
1027 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1028 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1029 | 
1030 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3
1031 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredGetPropType(t, info, n, rhsValue);
1032 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1033 |         // Determining type for #4
1034 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1035 |       }
1036 | 
1037 |       if (valueType == null) {
1038 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1039 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1040 |               n, t.getInput().isExtern(), ownerName));
1041 |         } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1042 |                    rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1043 |           // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1044 |           ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1045 |           if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1046 |             JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1047 |             String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1048 |             JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1049 |                 null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1050 |             if (delegateType != null &&
1051 |                 ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1052 |               defineSlot(n, parent, typeRegistry.getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1053 |                          true);
1054 |             }
1055 |           }
1056 |         }
1057 | 
1058 |         return;
1059 |       }
1060 | 
1061 |       boolean inferred = true;
1062 |       if (info != null) {
1063 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3
1064 |         inferred = !(info.hasType() || info.hasEnumParameterType() ||
1065 |             FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1066 |       }
1067 | 
1068 |       if (inferred) {
1069 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1070 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1071 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1072 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1073 |       }
1074 | 
1075 |       if (!inferred) {
1076 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1077 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1078 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1079 |           // declared yet.
1080 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput().isExtern();
1081 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1082 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1083 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1084 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1085 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1086 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern);
1087 |           }
1088 |         }
1089 | 
1090 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1091 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1092 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1093 |       }
1094 |     }

141 |   @Override
142 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     for (CallbackWrapper callback : callbacks) {

145 |     }

===== M0679 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 211-254
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
211 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
212 |     if (info != null) {
213 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
214 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
215 | 
216 |       // base type
217 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
218 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
219 |           baseType = ObjectType.cast(info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope));
220 |           if (baseType == null) {
221 |             reportWarning(EXTENDS_NON_OBJECT, fnName, baseType.toString());
222 |           }
223 |         } else {
224 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
225 |         }
226 |       }
227 | 
228 |       // implemented interfaces
229 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
230 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
231 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
232 |           ObjectType interType = ObjectType.cast(t.evaluate(scope));
233 |           if (interType != null) {
234 |             implementedInterfaces.add(interType);
235 |           } else {
236 |             reportError(BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, fnName);
237 |           }
238 |         }
239 |         if (baseType != null) {
240 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
241 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
242 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
243 |             Iterables.addAll(
244 |                 implementedInterfaces,
245 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
246 |           }
247 |         }
248 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
249 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
250 |       }
251 |     }
252 | 
253 |     return this;
254 |   }

===== M0688 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1604-1614
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1604 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1605 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1606 |     int len = s.length();
1607 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1608 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1609 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1610 |         return false;
1611 |       }
1612 |     }
1613 |     return true;
1614 |   }

===== M0693 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 232-260
Documentation:

Method code:
232 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
233 |     maybeEndStatement();
234 | 
235 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 | 
238 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
239 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
240 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
241 |       append(" ");
242 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
243 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
244 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
245 |       append(" ");
246 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
247 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
248 |       append(" ");
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
252 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
253 | 
254 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
255 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
256 |     // because it looks strange.
257 |     if (binOp) {
258 |       maybeCutLine();
259 |     }
260 |   }

===== M0708 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodingConventionAnnotator.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodingConventionAnnotator.java
Original range: 55-106
Documentation:

Method code:
55 |   @Override
56 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
57 |     int nType = n.getType();
58 |     switch (nType) {
59 |       case Token.NAME:
60 |       case Token.STRING:
61 |         String nString = n.getString();
62 |         if (nType == Token.NAME &&
63 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL &&
64 |             "eval".equals(nString)) {
65 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
66 |         }
67 |         if (convention.isConstant(nString)) {
68 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
69 |         }
70 |         break;
71 | 
72 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
73 |         JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
74 |         if (fnInfo == null) {
75 |           // Look for the info on other nodes.
76 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
77 |             // on ASSIGNs
78 |             fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
79 |           } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
80 |             // on var NAME = function() { ... };
81 |             fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
82 |           }
83 |         }
84 | 
85 |         // Compute which function parameters are optional and
86 |         // which are var_args.
87 |         Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
88 |         for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
89 |              arg != null;
90 |              arg = arg.getNext()) {
91 |           String argName = arg.getString();
92 |           JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
93 |               null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
94 | 
95 |           if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
96 |               typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
97 |             arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
98 |           }
99 |           if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
100 |               typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
101 |             arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
102 |           }
103 |         }
104 |         break;
105 |     }
106 |   }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 121-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
121 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
122 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
123 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
124 |     // show up in the type registry.
125 |     Scope newScope = null;
126 |     if (parent == null) {
127 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
128 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
129 | 
130 |       GlobalScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
131 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
132 |       scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
133 | 
134 |       // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
135 |       // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
136 |       // build properties on.
137 |       for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
138 |         JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
139 |         if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
140 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
141 |           ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
142 |           if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
143 |             NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
144 |                 scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
145 |           }
146 |         }
147 |       }
148 | 
149 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyProperties(
150 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyMap);
151 |     } else {
152 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
153 |       (new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope)).build();
154 |     }
155 |     typeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(newScope);
156 |     return newScope;
157 |   }

===== M0726 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 804-831
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
804 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
805 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
806 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
807 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
808 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
809 |           return;
810 |         }
811 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
812 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
813 | 
814 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
815 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
816 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
817 |           } else {
818 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
819 |           }
820 |         } else { // Has a catch.
821 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
822 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
823 |             return;
824 |           } else {
825 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
826 |           }
827 |         }
828 |         lastJump = handler;
829 |       }
830 |     }
831 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1215-1217
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an anonymous function? An anonymous function is one that
   * has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of anonymous functions:
   * <pre>
   * function () {}
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> anonymous:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A FUNCTION node
   * @return Whether n is an anonymous function
   */

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 107-144
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
107 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
108 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
109 |                 Scope functionScope, Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
110 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
111 |     this.compiler = compiler;
112 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
113 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
114 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
115 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
116 | 
117 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
118 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
119 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
120 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
121 |       }
122 |     }
123 | 
124 |     Iterator<Var> varIt = functionScope.getVars();
125 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
126 |       Var var = varIt.next();
127 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
128 |         continue;
129 |       }
130 | 
131 |       // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
132 |       // type is VOID.
133 |       if (var.getParentNode() != null &&
134 |           var.getType() == null && // no declared type
135 |           var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
136 |           !var.isExtern()) {
137 |         this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
138 |             var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
139 |       }
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
143 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
144 |   }

===== M0744 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1249-1281
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1249 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1250 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1251 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1252 |     // merge to fix this.
1253 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1254 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1255 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1256 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1257 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1258 | 
1259 |       if (value != null) {
1260 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1261 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1262 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1263 | 
1264 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1265 |         if (info == null) {
1266 |           info = varInfo;
1267 |         }
1268 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1269 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1270 |           // scope creation logic.
1271 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1272 |               t, value,  info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope()));
1273 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1274 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1275 |         } else {
1276 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1277 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1278 |         }
1279 |       }
1280 |     }
1281 |   }

Method code:
276 |     JSType getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String sourceName,
277 |         Node node, JSDocInfo info) {
278 |       JSType jsType = null;
279 |       Node objNode = node.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
280 |           node.getFirstChild() : null;
281 |       if (info != null) {
282 |         if (info.hasType()) {
283 |           jsType = info.getType().evaluate(scope);
284 |         } else if (FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
285 |           String fnName = node.getQualifiedName();
286 | 
287 |           // constructors are often handled separately.
288 |           if (info.isConstructor() && typeRegistry.getType(fnName) != null) {
289 |             return null;
290 |           }
291 | 
292 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
293 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(
294 |                   fnName, compiler, node, sourceName, scope)
295 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
296 |               .inferReturnType(info)
297 |               .inferParameterTypes(info)
298 |               .inferInheritance(info);
299 | 
300 |           // Infer the context type.
301 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
302 |           if (objNode != null) {
303 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
304 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
305 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
306 |               searchedForThisType = true;
307 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
308 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
309 |               searchedForThisType = true;
310 |             }
311 |           }
312 | 
313 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
314 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
315 |           }
316 | 
317 |           jsType = builder.buildAndRegister();
318 |         }
319 |       }
320 |       return jsType;
321 |     }

130 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n)

/**
     * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
     * each property separated by dots.  If the node ultimately under the left
     * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
     *
     * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
     * of the name and properties.
     */
Method code:
1674 |     public String getQualifiedName() {
1675 |         if (type == Token.NAME) {
1676 |             return getString();
1677 |         } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1678 |             String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1679 |             if (left == null) {
1680 |               return null;
1681 |             }
1682 |             return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1683 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1684 |              return "this";
1685 |         } else {
1686 |             return null;
1687 | 
1688 |         }
1689 | 
1690 |     }

===== M0775 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 770-784
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
770 |   private static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
771 |     switch (n.getType()) {
772 |       case Token.DO:
773 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
774 |       case Token.FOR:
775 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
776 |           return n;
777 |         }
778 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
779 |       case Token.LABEL:
780 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
781 |       default:
782 |         return n;
783 |     }
784 |   }

106 |     @Override
107 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {
108 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!values.isEmpty());
109 |       int size = values.size();
110 |       if (size == 1) {
111 |         return values.get(0);

===== M0779 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 421-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
421 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
422 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
423 | 
424 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
425 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
426 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
427 |       return 0;
428 |     }
429 | 
430 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
431 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
432 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
433 |       // a new statistic may have been added
434 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
435 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
436 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
441 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
442 |       return 0;
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
446 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
447 |   }

===== M0805 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 394-438
Documentation:

Method code:
394 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
395 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
396 |       cursor++;
397 |       --ungetCursor;
398 |       if (charno == -1) {
399 |         charno = getOffset();
400 |       }
401 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     for(;;) {
405 |       int c;
406 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
407 |         hitEOF = true;
408 |         if (charno == -1) {
409 |           charno = getOffset();
410 |         }
411 |         return EOF_CHAR;
412 |       }
413 |       cursor++;
414 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
415 | 
416 | 
417 |       if (c <= 127) {
418 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
419 |           lineEndChar = c;
420 |           c = '\n';
421 |         }
422 |       } else {
423 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
424 |           continue;
425 |         }
426 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
427 |           lineEndChar = c;
428 |           c = '\n';
429 |         }
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       if (charno == -1) {
433 |         charno = getOffset();
434 |       }
435 | 
436 |       return c;
437 |     }
438 |   }

===== M0815 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 254-304
Documentation:

Method code:
254 |   @Override
255 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
256 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
257 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
258 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
259 |         return true;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
263 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
264 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
265 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
266 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
267 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
268 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
269 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
270 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
271 |         return false;
272 |       }
273 | 
274 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
275 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
276 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
277 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
278 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
279 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
280 |             return false;
281 |           }
282 |         }
283 | 
284 |         return true;
285 |       }
286 | 
287 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
288 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
289 | 
290 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
291 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
292 |           return false;
293 |         }
294 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
295 |       }
296 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
297 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
298 |           return false;
299 |         }
300 |       }
301 |       return true;
302 |     }
303 |     return false;
304 |   }

===== M0825 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 136-163
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
136 |   JSType build() {
137 |     if (result == null) {
138 |       if (isAllType) {
139 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
140 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
141 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
142 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
143 |         } else {
144 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
145 |         }
146 |       } else {
147 |         Set<JSType> alternateSet = Sets.newUnmodifiableHashSet(alternates);
148 |         int size = alternateSet.size();
149 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
150 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
151 |         } else {
152 |           if (size > 1) {
153 |             result = new UnionType(registry, alternateSet);
154 |           } else if (size == 1) {
155 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
156 |           } else {
157 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
158 |           }
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 |     }
162 |     return result;
163 |   }

===== M0834 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.define(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 333-401
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines variable(s) or modifies types based on the content of the node
     * {@code n}. A variable definition creates variables in the current scope,
     * a function definition creates a binding, and an assignment updates the
     * type of the namespaces on which the definition is made (e.g.
     * {@code goog.FOO = 6}).
     * @param n a {@link Token#VAR}, {@link Token#FUNCTION} or
     *     {@link Token#ASSIGN} node
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     */
Method code:
333 |     void define(Node n, Node parent) {
334 |       Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
335 | 
336 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
337 |       switch (n.getType()) {
338 |         case Token.CATCH:
339 |           Node catchName = n.getFirstChild();
340 |           defineSlot(catchName, n, null);
341 |           break;
342 | 
343 |         case Token.VAR:
344 |           if (n.getChildCount() > 1) {
345 |             if (info != null) {
346 |               // multiple children
347 |               compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, MULTIPLE_VAR_DEF));
348 |             }
349 |             for (Node name : n.children()) {
350 |               defineName(name, n, parent, name.getJSDocInfo());
351 |             }
352 |           } else {
353 |             Node name = n.getFirstChild();
354 |             defineName(name, n, parent,
355 |                 (info != null) ? info : name.getJSDocInfo());
356 |           }
357 |           break;
358 | 
359 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
360 |           int parentType = parent.getType();
361 |           Preconditions.checkState(
362 |               (scope.isLocal() || parentType != Token.ASSIGN) &&
363 |               parentType != Token.NAME,
364 |               "function defined as standalone function when it is being " +
365 |               "assigned");
366 |           String functionName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
367 |           FunctionType functionType = getFunctionType(functionName, n, info,
368 |               null);
369 |           if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
370 |             defineSlot(n.getFirstChild(), n, functionType);
371 |           }
372 |           break;
373 | 
374 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
375 |           // TODO(nicksantos): We should support direct assignment to a
376 |           // prototype, as in:
377 |           // Foo.prototype = {
378 |           //   a: function() { ... },
379 |           //   b: function() { ... }
380 |           // };
381 |           // Right now (6/23/08), we understand most of this syntax, but we
382 |           // don't tie the "a" and "b" methods to the context of Foo.
383 | 
384 |           Node rvalue = n.getLastChild();
385 |           Node lvalue = n.getFirstChild();
386 |           info = (info != null) ? info : rvalue.getJSDocInfo();
387 |           if (rvalue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
388 |               info != null && info.isConstructor()) {
389 |             getFunctionType(lvalue.getQualifiedName(), rvalue, info,
390 |                 lvalue);
391 |           } else if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
392 |             lvalue.setJSType(
393 |                 getEnumType(lvalue.getQualifiedName(), n, rvalue,
394 |                     info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope)));
395 |           }
396 |           break;
397 | 
398 |         default:
399 |           throw new IllegalStateException(Integer.toString(n.getType()));
400 |       }
401 |     }

===== M0841 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 86-120
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param isIdeMode Whether in IDE mode, which affects the environment.
   * @param typeRegistry The type registry.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
86 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
87 |                            String sourceString,
88 |                            Config config,
89 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
90 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
91 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
92 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
93 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
94 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
95 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
96 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
97 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
98 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
99 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
100 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
101 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
105 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
106 |     try {
107 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
108 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
109 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
110 |     } finally {
111 |       Context.exit();
112 |     }
113 |     Node root = null;
114 |     if (astRoot != null) {
115 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
116 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
117 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
118 |     }
119 |     return root;
120 |   }

===== M0853 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 524-531
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
524 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, ObjectType owner) {
525 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
526 |     if (typeSet == null) {
527 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
528 |     }
529 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
530 |     typeSet.add(owner);
531 |   }

Method code:
566 |   public Scope getScope() {
567 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
568 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
569 |       return scope;
570 |     }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
180 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {
181 |     this.cfg = targetCfg;
182 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
183 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
184 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
185 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
186 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
187 |     } else {
188 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
189 |     }
190 |   }
