===== M0864 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 757-912
Documentation:
/**
     * Visit a node in the global scope, and add anything it declares to the
     * global symbol table.
     *
     * @param t The current traversal.
     * @param n The node being visited.
     * @param parent The parent of n
     */
Method code:
757 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
758 |       switch (n.getType()) {
759 |         case Token.CALL:
760 |           SubclassRelationship relationship =
761 |               codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
762 |           if (relationship != null) {
763 |             ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
764 |                 typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
765 |             ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
766 |                 typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
767 |             if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
768 |               FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
769 |               FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
770 | 
771 |               if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
772 |                 validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
773 |               }
774 | 
775 |               if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
776 |                 codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
777 |                     superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
778 |               }
779 |             }
780 |           }
781 | 
782 |           String singletonGetterClassName =
783 |               codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
784 |           if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
785 |             ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
786 |                 typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
787 |             if (objectType != null) {
788 |               FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
789 | 
790 |               if (functionType != null) {
791 |                 FunctionType getterType = new FunctionType(
792 |                     typeRegistry, null, null,
793 |                     new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build(),
794 |                     objectType, null);
795 |                 codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
796 |                                                       objectType);
797 |               }
798 |             }
799 |           }
800 | 
801 |           DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
802 |               codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
803 |           if (delegateRelationship != null) {
804 |             ObjectType delegatorObject = ObjectType.cast(
805 |                 typeRegistry.getType(delegateRelationship.delegator));
806 |             ObjectType delegateBaseObject = ObjectType.cast(
807 |                 typeRegistry.getType(delegateRelationship.delegateBase));
808 |             ObjectType delegateSuperObject = ObjectType.cast(
809 |                 typeRegistry.getType(
810 |                     codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName()));
811 |             if (delegatorObject != null &&
812 |                 delegateBaseObject != null &&
813 |                 delegateSuperObject != null) {
814 |               FunctionType delegatorCtor = delegatorObject.getConstructor();
815 |               FunctionType delegateBaseCtor =
816 |                   delegateBaseObject.getConstructor();
817 |               FunctionType delegateSuperCtor =
818 |                   delegateSuperObject.getConstructor();
819 | 
820 |               if (delegatorCtor != null && delegateBaseCtor != null &&
821 |                   delegateSuperCtor != null) {
822 |                 FunctionParamBuilder functionParamBuilder =
823 |                     new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
824 |                 functionParamBuilder.addRequiredParams(
825 |                     typeRegistry.getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE));
826 |                 FunctionType findDelegate = new FunctionType(
827 |                     typeRegistry, null, null, functionParamBuilder.build(),
828 |                     typeRegistry.createNullableType(delegateBaseObject), null);
829 | 
830 |                 FunctionType delegateProxy =
831 |                     typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
832 |                         delegateBaseObject.getReferenceName(), null, null,
833 |                         null);
834 |                 delegateProxy.setPrototypeBasedOn(delegateBaseCtor);
835 | 
836 |                 codingConvention.applyDelegateRelationship(
837 |                     delegateSuperObject, delegateBaseObject, delegatorObject,
838 |                     delegateProxy, findDelegate);
839 |                 delegateProxyMap.put(
840 |                     delegateProxy.getPrototype(),
841 |                     delegateBaseCtor.getPrototype());
842 |               }
843 |             }
844 |           }
845 | 
846 |           ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
847 |               codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
848 |           if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
849 |             ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
850 |                 typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
851 |             if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
852 |               objectLiteralCast.objectNode.setJSType(type);
853 |             } else {
854 |               compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
855 |                                            CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
856 |             }
857 |           }
858 |           break;
859 | 
860 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
861 |           if (!t.getInput().isExtern()) {
862 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
863 |           }
864 | 
865 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled separately.
866 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
867 |               parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
868 |             return;
869 |           }
870 |           define(n, parent);
871 |           break;
872 | 
873 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
874 |           // Handle constructor and enum definitions.
875 |           define(n, parent);
876 | 
877 |           // Handle typedefs.
878 |           checkForOldStyleTypedef(t, n);
879 | 
880 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
881 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
882 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
883 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
884 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
885 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
886 |           }
887 |           break;
888 | 
889 |         case Token.CATCH:
890 |           define(n, parent);
891 |           break;
892 | 
893 |         case Token.VAR:
894 |           define(n, parent);
895 | 
896 |           // Handle typedefs.
897 |           if (n.hasOneChild()) {
898 |             checkForOldStyleTypedef(t, n);
899 |             checkForTypedef(t, n.getFirstChild(), n.getJSDocInfo());
900 |           }
901 |           break;
902 | 
903 |         case Token.GETPROP:
904 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
905 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
906 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
907 |             checkForTypedef(t, n, n.getJSDocInfo());
908 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
909 |           }
910 |           break;
911 |       }
912 |     }

===== M0871 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 213-288
Documentation:

Method code:
213 |   @Override
214 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
215 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
216 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
217 | 
218 |     switch (n.getType()) {
219 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
220 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
221 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
222 |           return true;
223 |         }
224 |         return false;
225 |       case Token.TRY:
226 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
227 |         return true;
228 |     }
229 | 
230 |     /*
231 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
232 |      * is.
233 |      *
234 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
235 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
236 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
237 |      *
238 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
239 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
240 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
241 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
242 |      *
243 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
244 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
245 |      */
246 |     if (parent != null) {
247 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
248 |         case Token.FOR:
249 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
250 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
251 | 
252 |         // Skip the conditions.
253 |         case Token.IF:
254 |         case Token.WHILE:
255 |         case Token.WITH:
256 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
257 |         case Token.DO:
258 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
259 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
260 |         case Token.SWITCH:
261 |         case Token.CASE:
262 |         case Token.CATCH:
263 |         case Token.LABEL:
264 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
265 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
266 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
267 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
268 |         case Token.BREAK:
269 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
270 |         case Token.VAR:
271 |         case Token.RETURN:
272 |         case Token.THROW:
273 |           return false;
274 |         case Token.TRY:
275 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
276 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
277 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
278 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
279 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
280 |            */
281 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
282 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
283 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
284 |           }
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 |     return true;
288 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1017-1032
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
1017 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1018 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1019 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1020 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node, for instance,
1021 |     // is either part of an expression (as a anonymous function) or as
1022 |     // a statement.
1023 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1024 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1025 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1026 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1027 |       case Token.LABEL:
1028 |         return true;
1029 |       default:
1030 |         return false;
1031 |     }
1032 |   }

===== M0879 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 85-108
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
86 |       String sourceStr) {
87 |     try {
88 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
89 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
90 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |     } catch (IOException e) {
94 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.normalizeNodeTypes(root);
102 |       compiler.annotateCodingConvention(root);
103 |     }
104 | 
105 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
106 |     // the source file and module.
107 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
108 |   }

   * Declares a variable.
   *

Method code:
371 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,
372 |       JSType type, CompilerInput input, boolean inferred) {
